# #1073 — OmniRoute dispositions, 2026-08-01 through 2026-08-15 UTC

Reviewed 2026-11-20 against the retained, previously verified public source metadata in `/tmp/durindoor-1073-aug-eo1Czm` and the local DurinDoor tree. This record covers only merged PRs whose source `mergedAt` is in the inclusive UTC date window 2026-08-01 through 2026-08-15.

## Inventory and verification

The retained five-slice source inventory reports 352 PRs for Aug 1–7, 405 for Aug 8–14, and 21 for Aug 15: **778 rows**. The joined PR metadata contains 778 rows and 778 unique PR numbers; every merged timestamp is inside the requested bounds and every row has a merge commit oid. The table includes each upstream source link and exact available merge commit. Of the 778 source rows, **11 have incomplete changed-path lists** (reported GraphQL `totalCount` exceeds returned path nodes). Their exact per-row counts are retained below; no disposition is inferred from those partial lists.

Disposition labels are evidence-limited. Complete documentation-only, test-only, and CI/release-tooling-only path sets are separated from implementation surfaces. Provider sign-in paths are labeled Provider Sign-in. Every other row remains explicitly unresolved unless a cited pre-existing local port record establishes landed behavior. Titles or path similarity alone are not parity evidence. This row inventory does not select work for an owner.

## Reconciled focused August examples

The existing month-level report's focused source examples are #11921, #11814, and #11755; all merged Aug 30 and therefore fall outside this shard. No row in this window is treated as covered by those examples. The existing July report's examples are likewise outside this date range. No additional landed-coverage claim is made without an exact local port record and code evidence.

## Representative unresolved protocol cases reviewed

No final-source patch body was available in the retained metadata, and this task limits deeper review to reading final source and local contracts. Therefore I do not promote any unresolved row to a confirmed gap or acceptance finding from metadata alone. In particular, no implementation or owner-selection slate is recorded. All behavior-bearing rows below retain the precise unresolved reason that their change cannot be established from changed paths alone.
